=== RUN   TestParentCommands_KnownSubcommandStillWorks/session_list_help
List review sessions previously persisted to ~/.opencodereview/sessions/.
The session id printed here can be passed to 'ocr review --resume <id>'.

Usage:
  ocr session list [flags]

Aliases:
  list, ls

Flags:
  -h, --help          help for list
      --json          emit JSON instead of a table
      --limit int     cap the number of listed sessions (0 = unlimited) (default 20)
      --repo string   root directory of the git repository (default: current dir)

Global Flags:
      --color string   when to emit ANSI color: auto (only on terminal streams), always, or never (default "auto")
=== RUN   TestParentCommands_KnownSubcommandStillWorks/session_show_help
Find a saved session by id across repositories and show its metadata and per-file items.
Use --repo to restrict the lookup to one repository. Ambiguous ids are listed, not guessed at.

Usage:
  ocr session show [flags] <session-id>

Flags:
  -h, --help          help for show
      --json          emit JSON instead of a table
      --repo string   only look for the session under this repository (default: every repository)

Global Flags:
      --color string   when to emit ANSI color: auto (only on terminal streams), always, or never (default "auto")

=== RUN   TestParentCommands_KnownSubcommandStillWorks/delegate_preview_help
Outputs reviewable file list with mode/ref metadata for the host agent to construct git commands.

Usage:
  ocr delegate preview [flags]

Flags:
  -b, --background string        optional requirement/business context for the review
  -B, --background-file string   path to a Markdown file used as review background (takes precedence over --background)
  -c, --commit string            single commit hash or tag to review (vs its parent)
      --exclude string           comma-separated gitignore-style patterns to exclude; merged with rule.json excludes
  -f, --format string            output format: text or json (sarif is not supported by delegate mode) (default "text")
      --from string              source ref to start diff from (e.g., 'main')
  -h, --help                     help for preview
      --max-git-procs int        max concurrent git subprocesses (default 16)
      --repo string              root directory of the git repository (default: current dir)
      --rule string              path to JSON file with system review rules
      --to string                target ref to end diff at (e.g., 'feature-branch')

Global Flags:
      --color string   when to emit ANSI color: auto (only on terminal streams), always, or never (default "auto")
=== RUN   TestParentCommands_KnownSubcommandStillWorks/delegate_rule_help
Outputs resolved review rules grouped by content. Accepts multiple paths.

Usage:
  ocr delegate rule [flags] <path...>

Flags:
  -b, --background string        optional requirement/business context for the review
  -B, --background-file string   path to a Markdown file used as review background (takes precedence over --background)
  -c, --commit string            single commit hash or tag to review (vs its parent)
      --exclude string           comma-separated gitignore-style patterns to exclude; merged with rule.json excludes
  -f, --format string            output format: text or json (sarif is not supported by delegate mode) (default "text")
      --from string              source ref to start diff from (e.g., 'main')
  -h, --help                     help for rule
      --max-git-procs int        max concurrent git subprocesses (default 16)
      --repo string              root directory of the git repository (default: current dir)
      --rule string              path to JSON file with system review rules
      --to string                target ref to end diff at (e.g., 'feature-branch')

Global Flags:
      --color string   when to emit ANSI color: auto (only on terminal streams), always, or never (default "auto")

=== RUN   TestParentCommands_NoArgsPrintsHelp/config_no_args
Configuration management.

Examples:
  # Provider setup (interactive)
  ocr config provider
  ocr config model

  # Provider setup (non-interactive)
  ocr config set provider anthropic
  ocr config set model claude-opus-4-6
  ocr config set providers.anthropic.api_key "$ANTHROPIC_API_KEY"

  # Custom provider
  ocr config set provider my-gateway
  ocr config set custom_providers.my-gateway.url https://gateway.internal.com/v1
  ocr config set custom_providers.my-gateway.protocol openai

Usage:
  ocr config [flags]
  ocr config [command]

Available Commands:
  model       Interactive model selection
  provider    Interactive provider setup
  set         Set a configuration value
  unset       Remove a configuration value

Flags:
  -h, --help   help for config

Global Flags:
      --color string   when to emit ANSI color: auto (only on terminal streams), always, or never (default "auto")

Use "ocr config [command] --help" for more information about a command.

  ocr delegate [flags]
  ocr delegate [command]

Aliases:

=== RUN   TestSessionCompareCmd_HelpRoutes
Group the findings of two review sessions into new, persisting, resolved and not-reviewed.
Findings are matched on path, category and the offending snippet, so a finding that only moved down the file still counts as persisting.
Use --json for machine-readable output.

Usage:
  ocr session compare [flags] <before-session-id> <after-session-id>

Aliases:
  compare, diff

Flags:
  -h, --help          help for compare
      --json          emit the comparison as JSON
      --repo string   root directory of the git repository (default: current dir)

Global Flags:
      --color string   when to emit ANSI color: auto (only on terminal streams), always, or never (default "auto")
Group the findings of two review sessions into new, persisting, resolved and not-reviewed.
Findings are matched on path, category and the offending snippet, so a finding that only moved down the file still counts as persisting.
Use --json for machine-readable output.

Usage:
  ocr session compare [flags] <before-session-id> <after-session-id>

Aliases:
  compare, diff

Flags:
  -h, --help          help for compare
      --json          emit the comparison as JSON
      --repo string   root directory of the git repository (default: current dir)

Global Flags:
      --color string   when to emit ANSI color: auto (only on terminal streams), always, or never (default "auto")
